## 4. Accuris™ Mini Balances
- slug: accuris-mini-balances
- Item #: SCL-W1005-100
- subtitle/summary: Animal Weighing Scale
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/accuris-mini-balances/
- local content: Animal Weighing Scale Accuris™ Mini Balances Accuris™ Mini Balances are extraordinarily compact, easy to use, and convenient for a variety of laboratory weighing applications. With a footprint of only 2.6 x 3.9 inches, these Mini Balances offer exceptional accuracy and linearity through their full weighing ranges. Available with either a 100g or 500g capacity, these digital balances feature an easy to read, backlit, liquid crystal display. Buttons for ON/OFF, MODE and TARE are located on the front for easy access. The software includes a calibration function for easy in-lab calibration with a 100g or 500g calibration weight. Calibration weights sold separately. Designed for portability, the Accuris Mini Balances are powered by 2 x AAA batteries (included). After one minute sitting idle, the auto shut off feature turns off power to conserve battery life. Ideal for lab or field use, each balance includes a snap on protective cover with instructions conveniently printed on the underside. ## 5. Acrylic Brain Matrices
- slug: acrylic-brain-matrices
- Item #: RBMA-200C
- subtitle/summary: Microvette® 100 Blood Collection Tubes
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/acrylic-brain-matrices/
- local content: Microvette® 100 Blood Collection Tubes Acrylic Brain Matrices Brain matrices were developed to provide medical researchers with the ability to precisely prepare discrete regions in fresh or preserved tissue samples. Acrylic brain matrices are offered as durable and economical devices to prepare your tissue samples. Acrylic matrices are not recommended for heat or cold transfer and will become brittle if cooled or frozen. We have selected our matrices to section tissue slices to a thickness as small as approximately 1 mm, depending upon what matrix design you choose to use. Ordinary industrial razor blades are generally used to slice the brain and/or tissue material. We recommend that the cutting blade be wetted with saline to act as a lubricant before shearing the target tissue. Micron tissue punches are also available for removal of specific areas of the tissues. The coronal RBMs have the additional feature of a mid-line sagittal cut to facilitate the splitting of the left and right hemispheres. Sections can be as fine as 1 mm. ## 7. Aeroneb® Lab Control Module
- slug: aeroneb-lab-control-module
- Item #: NEB-7000
- subtitle/summary: Aeroneb® Lab Nebulizer
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/aeroneb-lab-control-module/
- local content: Aeroneb® Lab Nebulizer Aeroneb® Lab Control Module Category: Nebulizers The Aeroneb® Lab Nebulizer uses a high-efficiency aerosolization technology for use in pre-clinical aerosol research and inhalation studies, providing a valuable link between pre-clinical and clinical product development. Using Aerogen’s OnQ™ Aerosol Generator, the Aeroneb is designed to produce a highly respirable aerosol of virtually any drug in liquid. It produces a fine droplet, low-velocity aerosol without compressed air, and integrates easily into existing exposure and aerosol testing equipment. Aeroneb is a registered trademark of Nektar Therapeutics. What you get on day one Maintain molecular integrity Aerosolizes a broad range of liquid formulations without altering or damaging the compound’s molecular integrity Nebulizer and components are latex free Bridge preclinical and clinical studies Utilizes the same OnQ technology used in two commercially available nebulizer products, providing a bridge from preclinical to clinical studies Includes a universal adapter, good from 100V to 240V Produces a high-quality respirable aerosol with extremely low residual volume Note: The nebulizer delivery system requires the use of a flow regulated air source such as a pump or compressed gas source (not included) to move the aerosol through the tubing towards the animal. Standard tubing length is 36″. Please let us know the required tubing length for your MRI applications when ordering. The VetFlo™ reorder codes previously had an ACV prefix. The products remain the same, only the reorder codes were changed. Need replacement diaphragms? Buy them here. ## 15. Anesthetic Depth Indicator
- slug: anesthetic-depth-indicator
- Item #: ANEST-IND
- subtitle/summary: Anesthesia Masks Breathing Circuits for Traditional Vaporizers
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/anesthetic-depth-indicator/
- local content: Anesthesia Masks Breathing Circuits for Traditional Vaporizers Anti-Spill Device for Funnel Filled Vaporizers Anesthetic Depth Indicator A surgical plane of anesthetic depth is often defined by the loss of pedal reflex and confirmed by applying a stimulus to the paw pad. This anesthetic depth indicator is a sensory evaluator that standardizes common “toe-pinch” methods and is a good indicator of adequate anesthetic depth prior to surgery or other procedures.

## 20. AniPill
- slug: anipill
- Item #: 
- subtitle/summary: Stress-free core body temperature monitoring for animal research
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/anipill/
- local content: Stress-free core body temperature monitoring for animal research Anipill is available for sale in the US and Canada only. Our team can help you with product details, configurations, and quotes. Contact Us What you get with Anipill Continuous, high-precision data ±0.1 °C accuracy, 1- to 60-min sampling No daily restraint One-time dosing keeps animals calm and datasets clean Up to 8 animals per logger Scale studies without extra gear 20 days–10 months battery Choose the sampling rate that fits your protocol 3Rs-aligned Reduce stress, refine technique, replace surgery where possible How It Works Activate – Wake the capsule with the Activator box and pair it to the logger. Dose once – Oral gavage for larger species; small sub-Q pocket for mice. Record & retrieve – Live feed + 2 000-point onboard FIFO memory—so you never lose data, even out of range. Applications Pharmacology & Vaccinology – track fever kinetics in challenge studies. Biological-rhythm research – map circadian-core-temp cycles. Thermoregulation studies – quantify responses to environmental stressors. Hibernation & torpor – capture deep-sleep temperature shifts. Meets today’s animal-welfare guidelines. By replacing surgical telemetry in many studies, Anipill helps labs hit 3Rs goals while improving data integrity. - local content: CODA® Cuff Storage Container CODA® High Throughput Cuff Kits CODA® Data Acquisition Software If you are interested in analyzing your data, tracking animals or groups of animals, or assigning animals to specific researchers or technicians, you can upgrade the CODA® Monitor to include the CODA Data Acquisition Software. Your monitor can be upgraded at any time. Note: This software comes standard with the CODA High Throughput System. The CODA Data Acquisition Software allows you to: Compatibility Store and save all of your data Save each reading in both numeric and graphical representation Easily create experiment summary reports Identify and group animals Assign animals to researchers Accept or reject blood pressure measurements after the experiment The CODA Data Acquisition Software is compatible with Windows® 7, 8 & 10, 32 bit or 64 bit. ## 45. Ear Tag Applicator
- slug: ear-tag-applicator-with-spring-1
- Item #: TAG-APP
- subtitle/summary: Stamped Ear Tags
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/ear-tag-applicator-with-spring-1/
- local content: Stamped Ear Tags La Pias Ear Tags Extra Backs Ear Tag Applicator All of our animal identification ear tags are applied with this stainless steel hand-held ear tag applicator. Designed to seal the ear tags with minimal pressure, while providing a good, secure seal, it can be used with or without the spring, which applies slight closing pressure to help hold the tag in the applicator prior to sealing. Directions for use Ear tags should be washed in alcohol or disinfectant to help prevent infection to the ear. Place the mouse or rat ear tag in the ear tag applicator, keeping the “hole” side with the identification number flat against the jaw of the ear tag applicator. Locate the tag on the ear, applying behind a ring of cartilage. After crimping, be sure the tag point has come through the hole and is bent over, securing the ear tag properly. Applicator hints Place the tag in the applicator keeping the “hole” side of the tag flat against the jaw of the applicator with the indentation. Locate the tag within the ear, applying beyond a ring of cartilage. After sealing, be sure to check that the tag point has come through the hole and bent around, sealing properly. ## 49. Endotracheal Tubes

- local content: Endotracheal Intubation Kits Fiber Optic Lighting Assembly Endotracheal Tubes Each endotracheal tube includes a safety wedge, designed to assist in insertion of the catheter into the trachea and minimize the chances of inserting the cannula too deeply. During intubation, the wedge will guide the catheter tip to the proper location between the larynx and carina. ## 57. Flat Bottom Rodent Holders
- slug: flat-bottom-rodent-holders

- local content: Elizabethan Collars MiniARCO® Clipper Kit Flat Bottom Rodent Holders Our low-cost, high-quality economy line of rodent holders is very useful for procedures requiring access to the tail, such as when performing simple injections and blood sampling. To use, remove the tailgate and position the restrainer so the open end makes a 45° angle with the tabletop. Secure the rodent by the base of the tail and gently guide into the restrainer, forelimbs first. Apply gentle pressure to prevent the rodent from backing out of the restrainer. Once positioned, replace the tailgate into the groove to hold the animal in place. If you are looking for CODA® Animal Holders, ideal for non-invasive blood pressure measurement and enhanced access to the tail, click here. - local content: Far Infrared Warming Pads with Controller RightTemp® Jr. HTP-1500 Heat Therapy Pump The HTP-1500 Heat Therapy Pump allows you to safely warm your animals. This is one of the most accurate localized heat therapy system available. Its bright and easy-to-read display provides continuous real-time temperature readings. The digital controller uses proprietary software to manage your prescribed set-point temperature to within 1.0°F. Three temperature safety limits guard against the possibility of overheating and the hi-limit switches can be tested without taking the unit apart! 8′ hose and couplings included. This product is available in 110V configuration and only in the continental United States. Pump connections are modified for compatibility with our TPZ-814EA warming pads. Interested in better warming control using infrared heat? Click here. What you get on day one Maintain temperature within 1°F for animal safety Mercury-free system decreases potential hazards Uses tap water for added convenience Add-ons Animal warming pads and blankets (available separately) are available in several sizes to match animal body shape and warming needs. ## 103. Mouse Retractor Set
- slug: mouse-retractor-set
- Item #: SURGI-5001
- subtitle/summary: For the SurgiSuite surgical platform
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/mouse-retractor-set/
- local content: For the SurgiSuite surgical platform Set includes "> 2 magnetic fixators, 3 cm tall with 2 locking jaws) "> 1 elastomer positioner, 1 meter "> 10 wire retractors, 10 cm "> 10 retractor tips, as follows: 2 sharp tips, 1 mm 2 blunt tips, 1 mm 2 blunt tips, 2.5 mm 2 blunt tips, 5 mm 2 blunt tips, 7.5 mm Directions for use Retractor wires engage and lock into the fixator jaws at the desired elevation, allowing both linear and rotational adjustment over the entire length of the wire. One end of the wire is formed in a loop to provide grip for a gloved hand, and the other end is formed into a right angle hook that can retract on its own or lock to a range of retractor tips. The wires can also be reformed to create restraints or engage other lab apparatus that need to be secured in the surgical field. Retractor tips can be locked onto a wire for static retraction, or affixed to an elastic wire to form a dynamic retractor. Retractor tips and wires may be cold sterilized and autoclaved. The elastomer should not be cold sterilized and may degrade if exposed to other chemical solvents. Need Help With Your Order? ## 110. Non-Contact Infrared Thermometer with Laser Sight
- slug: non-contact-infrared-thermometer-with-laser-sight
- Item #: CODA-IRT
- subtitle/summary: MouseSTAT® Pulse Oximeter Whole Body Sensor for Mouse Pups
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/non-contact-infrared-thermometer-with-laser-sight/
- local content: MouseSTAT® Pulse Oximeter Whole Body Sensor for Mouse Pups Pulse Oximeter Paw Sensors MRI Compatible Non-Contact Infrared Thermometer with Laser Sight The Non-Contact Infrared Thermometer comfortably fits in the palm of your hand with a raised button on the top. It has a measurement range of -22℉ to 716℉ and is accurate within ±2% of reading. Emissivity is fixed at 0.95. Measures Centigrade and Fahrenheit. *Color may vary What you get on day one Accurately measures skin temperature, which can be expected to differ from body temperature based on the rodent’s thermoregulatory state and physiological status. Ideal for monitoring tail temperature when measuring blood pressure non-invasively. Specifications Directions for use Measuring range: -22℉ to 716℉ IR accuracy: ±2% of reading or ±4℉ Units: Centigrade or Fahrenheit Emissivity: Factory set to 0.95, fixed value Distance/target ratio: 8:1 (1" target diameter at 8" distance) Battery: 9v battery, included To measure tail temperature, position the infrared thermometer approximately one inch away from the base of the tail, making sure the light is shining on the tail, not on the warming platform. Need Help With Your Order? Our product specialists are here to help with additional information on our products, grant proposals, orders and more. Give us a call today! Call 888-572-8887 Specifications

## 120. Rat Retractor Set
- slug: rat-retractor-set
- Item #: SURGI-5002
- subtitle/summary: For the SurgiSuite surgical platform
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/rat-retractor-set/
- local content: For the SurgiSuite surgical platform Set includes "> 2 magnetic fixators, 6 cm tall with 4 locking jaws) "> 1 elastomer positioner, 1 meter "> 10 wire retractors, 14 cm "> 10 retractor tips, as follows: 2 sharp tips, 1 mm 2 blunt tips, 1 mm 2 blunt tips, 2.5 mm 2 blunt tips, 5 mm 2 blunt tips, 7.5 mm Directions for use Retractor wires engage and lock into the fixator jaws at the desired elevation, allowing both linear and rotational adjustment over the entire length of the wire. One end of the wire is formed in a loop to provide grip for a gloved hand, and the other end is formed into a right angle hook that can retract on its own or lock to a range of retractor tips. The wires can also be reformed to create restraints or engage other lab apparatus that need to be secured in the surgical field. Retractor tips can be locked onto a wire for static retraction, or affixed to an elastic wire to form a dynamic retractor. Retractor tips and wires may be cold sterilized and autoclaved. The elastomer should not be cold sterilized and may degrade if exposed to other chemical solvents. Need Help With Your Order? ## 148. Stainless Steel Brain Matrices
- slug: stainless-steel-brain-matrices
- Item #: RBMS-200C
- subtitle/summary: Microvette® Prepared Micro Tubes
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/stainless-steel-brain-matrices/
- local content: Microvette® Prepared Micro Tubes Microvette® 200 Blood Collection Tubes Stainless Steel Brain Matrices Brain matrices were developed to better help the medical research community prepare discrete regions in fresh or preserved tissue samples. The more durable stainless steel matrices are useful when heat transfer or the maintenance of a specific temperature range is required in your scientific protocol. We have selected our matrices to section tissue slices to a thickness as small as approximately 0.5 mm (sections can vary from 0.5 to 0.75 mm), depending upon what brain matrix design you choose to use. Ordinary industrial razor blades are generally used to slice the brain and/or tissue material. We recommend that the cutting blade be wetted with saline to act as a lubricant before shearing the target tissue. Micron tissue punches are also available for removal of specific areas of the tissues. The coronal RBMs have the additional feature of a mid-line sagittal cut to facilitate the splitting of the left and right hemispheres. Sections can be as fine as 1 mm. Repeatable slices to 0.5 mm providing precise blocking prior to sectioning Can be chilled or sterilized Size Coronal Slices, 0.5 mm (For mice 40 to 75g)Sagittal Slices, 0.5 mm (For mice 40 to 75g)Coronal Slices, 1 mm (For mice 40 to 75g)Sagittal Slices, 1 mm (For mice 40 to 75g)Coronal Slices, 1 mm (For rats 175 to 300g)Sagi…

The ear tags are silver in color and made of a monel, nickel-copper alloy with excellent corrosion resistance, strength and ductility. Note: Numbering longer than 3 characters will wrap around to the other side of the tag. Custom orders A minimum order of 1,000 ear tags is required for special numbering. Ear tag sequence always begins at 1, examples include 3001 to 4000, 4001 to 5000. If you require ear tag numbering that begins at “0” please specify on your order. Note that it may take additional time for processing. Custom numbering and alpha characters incur additional fees. Please contact Customer Service at 888-572-8887 for a quotation. Allow 10 to 14 weeks for delivery. Once positioned, replace the nosepiece into the groove and tighten with the screw. - local content: RightTemp® Sensor for Animal or Warming Pad RightTemp® Type JKT Thermocouple Meter The precalibrated, single-Input Digi-Sense Type J/K/T Thermocouple Thermometer has an easy-to-use control panel and Min/Max and hold functions. It features offset adjustment, °F and °C selection, backlit display, low-battery indicator and auto-shutoff. The RET-2 (rat) and RET-3 (mouse) rectal probes are compatible with this thermocouple meter. What you get on day one Reliable measurements Precalibrated to NIST-traceable standards Electronic offset function allows compensation of probe errors Includes Specifications Soft carrying case Protective rubber armor boot with built-in stand One 9 V battery NIST-traceable calibration report with data supplied by InnoCal Range Type J: -200 to 1000°C Type K: -250 to 1372°C Type T: -250 to 400°C Resolution 0.1°C from -299.9.9 to 299.9.9°C 1°C outside this range Accuracy ±0.25% of reading plus 1°C for temp &lt;-99.9°C ±0.25% of reading plus 0.5°C for temp &gt;-99.9°C Input connector Standard miniconnector Display Single line LCD Operating temp 0 to 50°C Power (4) 1.5V AAA batteries Probe Use any Type J, K, or T thermocouple probe Dimensions 14 cm W x 3.5 cm H x 7 cm D Need Help With Your Order? Tec 3 Funnel filled Size Vaporizer for Isoflurane, 125mlVaporizer for Isoflurane with Extra Capacity, 250mlChoose an optionVaporizer for Isoflurane, 125mlVaporizer for Isoflurane with Extra Capacity, 250mlClear Why use a new vaporizer? For your safety, the safety of your animals and the integrity of your study, Kent Scientific offers genuine, new vaporizers manufactured specifically for either isoflurane or sevoflurane. Although remanufactured or reconditioned vaporizers cost less, there have been reports of poor performance related to how the vaporizers are actually “remanufactured.” It is very costly to truly recondition a vaporizer to a specific agent and if not done properly, it could result in unknowingly overdosing your animal. You may also run into problems with annual calibration if your vaporizer was not reconditioned properly. Accessories Optional induction chambers and anesthesia masks are available for purchase, or select a convenient, cost-effective anesthesia starter kit that includes all the components necessary for your setup. ## 170. VetFlo™ Four Channel Anesthesia Stand
- slug: vetflo-four-channel-anesthesia-stand
- Item #: VETFLO-1220
- subtitle/summary: Vaporizer for Sevoflurane Key Filled

- local content: Vaporizer for Sevoflurane Key Filled VetFlo™ Single Channel Anesthesia Stand VetFlo™ Four Channel Anesthesia Stand Vaporizer sold separately. Kent Scientific’s small animal and rodent anesthesia machine is one of the easiest to use and most economical gas anesthesia systems on the market. Perfect for laboratory and veterinary anesthesia delivery, the VetFlo™ anesthesia system is completely self-contained and portable. The multi-channel base unit is designed for multiple stations that are individually controlled, operating off of one vaporizer. With an easy-to-follow chart, each station can deliver a different flow rate of anesthesia. Dimensions: 13″ H x 14.625″ W x 10″ D We are making improvements to the design of this product and a scheduled release date will be determined soon. Kent Scientific’s small animal and rodent anesthesia machine is one of the easiest to use and most economical gas anesthesia systems on the market. Perfect for laboratory and veterinary anesthesia delivery, the VetFlo™ anesthesia system is completely self-contained and portable. The multi-channel base unit is designed for multiple stations that are individually controlled, operating off of one vaporizer. With an easy-to-follow chart, each station can deliver a different flow rate of anesthesia. ## 174. VetFlo™ Vaporizer
- slug: vaporizer-with-vetflo-single-channel-anesthesia-stand
- Item #: N/A
- subtitle/summary: Traditional single channel anesthesia system
- source: https://www.kentscientific.com/products/vaporizer-with-vetflo-single-channel-anesthesia-stand/
- local content: Traditional single channel anesthesia system What you get from VetFlo Brand new vaporizer canisters Never reconditioned or remanufactured Superior design Durable stand, accurate, high-quality flowmeters, small footprint Complete systems Includes all the tubing and connectors needed Simple set-up and use Y-adapter tubing for easy transfer from induction chamber to anesthesia mask Why use a new vaporizer? Base system includes For your safety, the safety of your animals and the integrity of your study, Kent Scientific offers genuine, new vaporizers manufactured specifically for either isoflurane or sevoflurane. Although remanufactured or reconditioned vaporizers cost less, there have been reports of poor performance related to how the vaporizers are actually “remanufactured.” It is very costly to truly recondition a vaporizer to a specific agent and if not done properly, it could result in unknowingly overdosing your animal. You may also run into problems with annual calibration if your vaporizer was not reconditioned properly. Tabletop stand (13" H x 12" W x 10" D) One inlet flowmeter, 0 to 1000 mL (A 200 to 4000 mL flowmeter is available in both single and multi-station systems, except on 6-channel systems) One set of inlet/outlet caps Charcoal filter canister High-pressure oxygen hose with DISS fittings The VetFlo Traditional Anesthesia System with isoflurane vaporizer includes…
